byte accounting = sum of per-entry `proto.Size`/`ByteSizeLong`, crossing entry INCLUDED (cap 1500 → 5-entry batches at ~356B); (BUF-2) defaults **`buffer_size_bytes` 16384** (the 47-entry size batch) / **`buffer_flush_interval` 1s** (the ~995ms cadence); surface UNCHANGED at **1189** (non-H2 **1185**); `logs_written` ++ per ENTRY (batch-invariant); (BUF-5) drain — flush the pending buffer on Close BEFORE `CloseAndRecv` (a faithful improvement over the reference's lossy per-worker `Canceled` drain). **The 44.2 design (ADR-0256 §Context drafted in SPEC §13):** lift `buffer_size_bytes`/`buffer_flush_interval` into `ALSConfig` (with the 16384/1s defaults) + the buffer machinery in `GrpcAccessLogSink.run()` (a `buf []*HTTPAccessLogEntry` + `bufBytes` running sum + a `time.NewTicker` flush-interval timer + a `flush()` closure firing on size-OR-timer + flush-on-channel-close) replacing the fixed one-entry-per-message flush + the `NewGrpcAccessLogSink` signature extension + the `main.go` pass-through + the driver-owned `accessloggrpc` receiver's per-message batch exposure (`BatchSizes`/`MaxBatchSize`/`MessageCount`) + the `0082-grpc-access-log-buffering` differential (cross-side aggregated 7-field payload + the subject-side `maxBatchSize >= 2` proof). **ALL COUNTS UNCHANGED at this SPEC commit (docs-only):** stat surface **1189** (H2 cluster; **lazy-establish** [stream opened on the first record] / **identifier-once** [`Identifier{Node, LogName}` on the first `Send` only, re-armed across reconnect] / **reconnect-on-error** [a `Send` error nils the stream + re-sends THIS record once; a second failure drops it, logged-not-counted] / **fixed simple flush** [one `HTTPAccessLogEntry` per `StreamAccessLogsMessage`] / a **bounded-`Close`** [closes the channel, waits `closeDrainGrace`=5s for the writer to drain + `CloseAndRecv`, then `cancel()`s the stream ctx to unwedge a stalled peer — deterministic network shutdown; the file sink can't hang, a network sink can]) over a typed `ALSClient` (the `AuthClient` shape — `NewALSClient`/`StreamAccessLogs`/idempotent `Close`, gated on cluster-exists + `UseH2()`) over the phase-18 `grpcclient.Dialer` (the layer's SECOND typed wrapper, confirming ADR-0158); - **prior active-phase:** `phase 43.2b (h2-connection-pool) IMPL done` (2026-06-24) — the 10-task TDD PLAN executed end-to-end subagent-driven (fresh implementer per task), the six-gate GREEN, the full **82-dir differential GREEN** (lifecycle-state 3 → 4; **row 43 (`connection-pooling`) FLIPS `in-progress → done`** — ALL legs 43.1 + 43.2a + 43.2b landed ⇒ the **Upstream-robustness family (phases 39–43) CLOSES**). As built: **ADR-0254** — graceful GOAWAY-driven HTTP/2 upstream connection rotation (the H2-multiplex-pool drain lifecycle ADR-0253's minimal liveness DEFERRED). The codec exposes three read-only accessors over the existing `goawayCh`/`ctx` — `GoneAway() bool` + `GoneAwayCh() <-chan struct{}` + `Done() <-chan struct{}` (NO `cc.cancel()`, so an in-flight stream below `LastStreamID` still completes; the `Done()` accessor REQUIRED because `cc.ctx` is unexported from package `cluster`). A GOAWAY'd conn becomes DRAINING: (1) **admission-skip** — the two ESTABLISHED-conn sites (`findStreamHitLocked` + `h2PromoteLocked`'s stream-slot branch) add `&& !pc.cc.GoneAway()`; (2) **in-flight drain-close-on-last-stream** — `makeRelease`'s eager-close generalizes `pc.cc.Closed() && inFlight==0` → `(Closed() || GoneAway()) && inFlight==0`; (3) **idle prompt-close** — a per-conn drain-watcher goroutine (`watchDrain`, spawned in `dialAndPool` after the pool append) `select`s `GoneAwayCh()` vs `Done()` and evicts an IDLE draining conn promptly under `h2PoolMu` (re-checking `findPooledLocked != nil` — the double-evict guard); (4) **lazy replacement** via the established MISS path (`upstream_cx_http2_total` ++; permit re-homed). The rotation is observed ENTIRELY via `upstream_cx_http2_total` + the base `upstream_cx_*` (NO new GOAWAY-named stat — AMEND-H2B-1; the reference has no `goaway_received` + reads `http2.goaway_sent==0` on a graceful drain-close). The FIRST codec→cluster stat wiring: `cluster.<name>.http2.rx_reset` + `http2.tx_reset` (useH2-gated, **1185 → 1187**) injected via the variadic `h2.WithResetHooks(onRx, onTx func())` option applied PRE-`readLoop` (D-H2B-WIRING — a setter would race the running readLoop; the codec stays stats-agnostic, no `internal/stats` import) at `dialPooledH2To`; the kept `DialH2` passes nil. The codec increments at its existing RST sites (`dispatchFrame` `RSTStreamFrame` rx + `RoundTrip` `ctx.Done` CANCEL tx). NEW BackendKind **38** (`H2GoawayResponder` — a raw-`golang.org/x/net/http2`-framer h2c backend with `/__release` + `/__goaway`[NO_ERROR] + `/__rst`[INTERNAL_ERROR] gates; the kind-37 `H2HoldResponder` uses `h2c.NewHandler`/`http.Server` which CANNOT emit on-demand GOAWAY/RST frames). Differential **`0080-h2-goaway-rotation`** (cross-side EXACT — a six-stage sleepless drive: establish ⇒ in-flight drain ⇒ idle drain ⇒ rx_reset prong ⇒ tx_reset prong ⇒ quiesce; cross-side EXACT `upstream_cx_http2_total==2` + `http2.rx_reset==1` + `http2.tx_reset==1`). **Two documented FAITHFUL fixture-drive departures (recorded in ADR-0254 — NOT behavior divergences):** (A) `/__release` broadened to a backend-global broadcast; (B) `tx_reset` driven via a `per_try_timeout` (envoy-go dispatches H2 downstream streams on the connection-level ctx, so a downstream cancel doesn't propagate to the upstream `RoundTrip` ctx — the 42.2b "honors ctx.Deadline not ctx.Done on the upstream" precedent; the `per_try_timeout` reaches the SAME codec CANCEL site, cross-side EXACT). **The per-conn drain-watcher's idle-close is a recorded COVERAGE BOUNDARY:** 3 of 4 `-count=1` deliberate breaks bit (admission-skip → `cx_http2_total`, rx_reset, tx_reset); the watcher idle-close break does NOT bite because `makeRelease`'s eager-close masks it (the `/__goaway` control request rides the target conn as an in-flight stream, so its release eager-closes the conn before the watcher acts). The watcher remains CORRECT + ESSENTIAL in production for the truly-idle-GOAWAY case and is RIGOROUSLY UNIT-proven (a 1000-iteration contested race test + an idle-prompt-close test); a watcher-isolating differential is a recorded hardening item. 20/20 flake-free + `-race`-clean. ADR-0254 §Decision/§Consequences landed IN-PLACE per ADR-0044. As-built counts: stat **1185 → 1187** (+2 — `http2.rx_reset` + `http2.tx_reset`, useH2-gated; non-H2 stays **1183**) / fixtures **81 → 82** (`0080-h2-goaway-rotation`) / fuzzers **42 documented → 43** (RECONCILED to the actual `^func Fuzz` count per `reference_fuzzer_count_docs_drift` — 43.2b adds none; D-H2B-FUZZER-RECONCILE) / BackendKind tail **37 → 38** (`H2GoawayResponder`) / DECISIONS tail **ADR-0253 → ADR-0254** (next-free **ADR-0255**); ZERO new packages + ZERO new go.mod modules. The full six-gate GREEN (build/vet/gofmt/lint clean; unit+integration GREEN; full 82-dir differential GREEN). **DEFERRED hardening items (recorded in ADR-0254):** `http2.goaway_sent` (would diverge — AMEND-H2B-3), `http2.stream_refused_errors` (reference-dormant), `min(local,peer)` peer-min enforcement, `nextStreamID` 2^31 retirement, + a watcher-isolating differential. **NEXT → a FRESH BRAINSTORM for a NEW subject** (the Upstream-robustness family is CLOSED — 0 candidates remain; the next phase opens a NEW family per the family-closure precedent; `superpowers:brainstorming`, a fresh worktree off master per `feedback_git_worktrees`). an H1 downstream → h2c upstream cluster `c_h2` with `max_concurrent_streams: 100`; a raw-`golang.org/x/net/http2`-framer h2c backend with `/goaway` + `/rst_last` control gates; sequenced single-shot + `/stats`-delta scrapes; ran in a throwaway worktree off master per `feedback_git_worktrees`, discarded after pinning). **THE HEADLINE EMPIRICAL REVERSAL (AMEND-H2B-1..4) — the probe REVERSED the brainstorm's stat scope DOWN:** (AMEND-H2B-1) a peer GOAWAY is observed ENTIRELY via the connection-lifecycle counters — `upstream_cx_close_notify` ++ IMMEDIATELY on receipt (drain mark), then for an IDLE conn a PROMPT local close (`upstream_cx_destroy`/`_destroy_local` ++, `cx_active` --) and for an IN-FLIGHT conn the conn HELD OPEN until the last in-flight stream completes then `upstream_cx_destroy_local` ++ (`_with_active_rq` STAYS 0 — graceful), replacement LAZY on the next request (`upstream_cx_http2_total` 1→2) — with **NO `http2.*` counter moving during a clean rotation** and **NO `goaway_received` counter existing** in the reference's 23-name `http2.*` subtree; (AMEND-H2B-2) of the brainstorm's FOUR candidate reset/goaway counters only **TWO** are LIVE + cross-side assertable — `http2.tx_reset` (downstream-cancel → the codec's existing `RoundTrip` ctx.Done `WriteRSTStream(id, CANCEL)`) + `http2.rx_reset` (backend RST → the codec's existing `dispatchFrame` `RSTStreamFrame` case → 503, conn survives) ⇒ register exactly those two, surface **1185 → 1187 (+2, REVISED DOWN from the brainstorm's ~1190/~5)**; (AMEND-H2B-3) `http2.goaway_sent` DEFERRED (the reference reads 0 through a full drain-close — envoy-go's `Close()`-emitted GOAWAY would DIVERGE non-zero) + `http2.stream_refused_errors` DEFERRED (reference-DORMANT: it proactively respects peer `SETTINGS_MAX_CONCURRENT_STREAMS` + opens a conn-per-concurrent-cold-request prefetch, so it never RECEIVES a REFUSED_STREAM ⇒ a cross-side EXACT assert would be vacuous-at-0); (AMEND-H2B-4) the `0080` differential is cross-side EXACT under the sequenced-barrier drive — NO subject-EXACT + cross-ROBUST fallback needed (`cx_http2_total==2` post-rotation, `rx_reset==1`/`tx_reset==1` on their dedicated prongs). **The design (ADR-0254):** the codec exposes `GoneAway() bool` + `GoneAwayCh() <-chan struct{}` (read-only over the existing `goawayCh` — NO `cc.cancel()`, so an in-flight stream below `LastStreamID` still completes); the two ESTABLISHED-conn admission sites add `&& !pc.cc.GoneAway()`; `makeRelease`'s eager-close generalizes `pc.cc.Closed() && inFlight==0` → `(Closed() || GoneAway()) && inFlight==0` (the in-flight drain-close-on-last-stream); As built: **ADR-0252** — `max_connections` HARD-CAP + `max_pending_requests` bounded wait-queue, the project's FIRST standing upstream request queue. A per-cluster DEFAULT-priority `connPool` (`internal/cluster/connpool.go` — a SIBLING to the phase-41 `circuitBreaker`; `pool *connPool` field on `circuitBreaker`) with a `sync.Mutex`-guarded `activeConns` counter + a FIFO of per-waiter BUFFERED cap-1 wake channels. A connection-creation permit is try-acquired at the two creation boundaries (`Cluster.Dial` raw-dial + the `AcquireH1` pool-MISS); a pool HIT reuses an already-counted conn (no permit). At the cap the request PENDS (bounded by `max_pending_requests`), woken via DIRECT permit-handoff on `connWithGauge.Close` (`connDec` → `pool.releaseConn` → pop head + send) or on `PutIdleH1` idle-return (CLOSE-and-wake when a waiter is queued, routing BOTH wake sources through the single permit mechanism); ctx-cancel re-locks + `removeWaiterLocked`, and if already dequeued drains + gives the permit back (raced-permit-never-leaks). Queue-full → `cluster.IsConnPoolOverflow` sentinel → fail-fast 503 + `upstream_rq_pending_overflow` (the shared phase-41 counter). **The load-bearing DEPARTURE (AMEND-CP1 — `reference_max_connections_soft_breaker`):** the reference's `max_connections` is a SOFT breaker (`cx_active` can exceed the cap with timing slop); envoy-go implements a CLEAN HARD-CAP as a documented departure recorded in ADR-0252. Byte-identical when no `circuit_breakers` (the full 79→80-dir differential is the regression anchor). **The 2 new stat shapes:** `upstream_rq_pending_active` gauge + `upstream_rq_pending_total` counter (+2 ⇒ **1181 → 1183**); 3 existing shapes (`cx_open`, `rq_pending_open`, `upstream_cx_overflow`) ACTIVATED from emit-0 (+0 surface delta — already registered). Differential **`0078-connection-pool-max-connections`** (cross-side; REUSE `BlockingHoldResponder` — NO new BackendKind; `refContainerListenerPort = 19167`): staged drive fill N=2 held → pend M=2 → overflow J=2 503s → sticky-release drain; subject-exact invariants + cross-side robustness (overflow delta ≥ 1, final `cx_open == 0`); 2 deliberate breaks (A: hard-cap disabled; B: queue-full disabled) + 20/20 flake-free + `-race`-clean. As built: **ADR-0251** — hedging (`RouteAction.hedge_policy` field 27 / `VirtualHost` field-17 fallback), the project's FIRST concurrent-upstream-attempt control plane. A SEPARATE concurrent `hedgeExecutorH1`/`hedgeExecutorH2` (`internal/filter/http/router/hedge.go` — a SIBLING to `retry.go`; NO existing executor modified) dispatched via the `H{1,2}ClusterAction` (+ weighted) closure switch — the hedge branch BEFORE the `if a.rp != nil` retry branch, gated by `(*HedgePolicy).TriggersConcurrency()`. Each in-flight attempt is a goroutine (body replay + `do{H1,H2}ClusterAction` + `matches` classify + the CB-admission/`RecordUpstreamResult`/`retry_budget` seams) with a per-attempt HEDGE-TRIGGER timer (`time.AfterFunc` at `per_try_timeout`) that on expiry launches a budget-counted hedge LEAVING the original in flight; a BUFFERED first-acceptable-wins collector returns the first non-retriable result + cancels/drains losers (no leak, `-race`-clean). The `hedge_policy` parsed in `parseHedgePolicy` (`hcm/config.go`) with the vhost fallback + carried on `clusterRouteAction.hedgePolicy` (`hcm/actions.go`); the `hp *HedgePolicy` threaded through the `H{1,2}ClusterAction` signatures. **The load-bearing invariants held as-built:** (AMEND-H1 — #1) a hedged per-try-timeout increments `upstream_rq_retry` (+ `_retry_backoff_exponential`, NO `time.Sleep`), NEVER `upstream_rq_per_try_timeout` (which STAYS **0** under hedge mode — the per-try-timeout is a hedge-LAUNCH not an abandonment; the hedge path uses a HEDGE-TRIGGER timer NOT the 42.2a cancelling-deadline-+-discriminator); (AMEND-H2) `upstream_rq_retry_limit_exceeded` under hedging = "no more hedges can launch" NOT a final failure — the in-flight original can still WIN with a 200 (hedging RECOVERS a slow request where 42.2a sequential ABANDONS it to a 504); (D-S422B-2 — a recorded departure) the H1/H2 loser-cancel ASYMMETRY [`doH1ClusterAction` honors only `ctx.Deadline()` ⇒ an H1 loser completes on backend-response (the `0077` `/__release` guarantees termination, NO active H1-loser socket reset); `doH2ClusterAction`'s `RoundTrip(ctx)` honors `ctx.Done()` ⇒ an H2 loser aborts promptly; the driver code UNCHANGED, reused concurrently]; (PLAN I1) the `time.AfterFunc` callback is a PURE non-blocking `hedgeReq <- struct{}{}` send, the COLLECTOR LOOP the SOLE mutator of `hedgesLaunched`/`limitExceededOnce` + SOLE caller of `Inc*`/`TryAcquireRetry`/`launch` (channels BUFFERED to `int(hp.InitialRequests)+maxHedges+1` ⇒ no loser blocks on send ⇒ no leak; `-race`-clean); (D-S422B-3/AMEND-H4) the `initial_requests`/`additional_request_chance` fan-out is reference-no-op'd ⇒ SUBJECT-SIDE (UNIT-tested — NO `0078` dir), each fan-out attempt `TryAcquireRetry`-gated BEFORE the goroutine spawn (acquire-gates-spawn caps the live goroutine count under the `retry_budget`); (AMEND-H5) NO new stat — surface STAYS **1181** (`EnsureRetryStats` is called when `TriggersConcurrency()` even with no `retry_policy`). **Byte-identical when no triggering `hedge_policy`** (a nil/non-triggering guard — the full 78→79-dir differential is the regression anchor). Differential **`0077-hedge-on-per-try-timeout`** (cross-side, H1-only; REUSE `BlockingHoldResponder` — NO new BackendKind): a SINGLE held host past a small `per_try_timeout` ⇒ original + N hedges launch concurrently; fire `/hedge` in a goroutine, poll-to-converge on `upstream_rq_retry==N && upstream_rq_retry_limit_exceeded==1` (the release barrier — NEVER a sleep), `/__release` ⇒ first-acceptable 200; cross-side EXACT [single host, offset-irrelevant]: `upstream_rq_retry==N`, **`upstream_rq_per_try_timeout==0`** (the AMEND-H1 keystone), `_retry_limit_exceeded==1`, `upstream_rq_total==N+1`, `downstream_rq_2xx==1`, final 200; 2 `-count=1` deliberate breaks (A: neutered hedge-launch ⇒ `upstream_rq_retry` never converges; B: `IncUpstreamRqPerTryTimeout` in the hedge path ⇒ the `per_try_timeout==0` assert fails) + 20/20 flake-free + `-race`-clean. `hedge_on_per_try_timeout` LEAVES the slow attempt in flight + launches a concurrent hedge (a hedge counts as a retry). **THE highest-risk pin — D-H-RACE (the NEW as-built wrinkle the parent could not foresee):** the collector's loser-cancellation must NOT trip the race-robust discriminator into a false synthesized-504/`IncUpstreamRqPerTryTimeout()` (the wall-clock fallback fires on deadline-elapse regardless of cancel cause; the `ctx.Err()==nil` parent-alive guard only suppresses when losers are cancelled via an ANCESTOR of the per-attempt ctx) — the 42.2b SPEC MUST pin a mitigation. **Split verification:** `hedge_on_per_try_timeout` CROSS-SIDE (`contrib-v1.37.2` implements it), the `initial_requests`/`additional_request_chance` fan-out SUBJECT-SIDE (a recorded departure; - **prior active-phase:** `phase 42.2a (per_try_timeout) IMPL done` (2026-06-21) — the 11-task TDD PLAN executed end-to-end subagent-driven (fresh implementer per task + two-stage review), the six-gate GREEN, the full **78-dir differential GREEN** (lifecycle-state 3 → 4; row 42 STAYS `in-progress`). As built: **ADR-0250** — `RetryPolicy.per_try_timeout` (field 3), a per-attempt deadline over the 42.1 retry loop; the project's FIRST request-scoped deadline (envoy-go does NOT implement the global `RouteAction.timeout`). The 42.1 `retryExecutorH1`/`retryExecutorH2` (`internal/filter/http/router/retry.go`) wrap each `do{H1,H2}ClusterAction` call in a child `context.WithTimeout(ctx, rp.perTryTimeout)` (when `>0`); on the child deadline expiring while the PARENT ctx is alive, the attempt is a per-try-timeout — a synthesized **504** (overriding the H1 driver's 502 / the H2 `Status:0` CANCEL sentinel), `IncUpstreamRqPerTryTimeout()`, retried via `perTryTimeoutRetriable()` (`matches(504,false) || retryReset`) under {5xx, gateway-error, retriable-status-codes∋504, **reset**} — NOT `connect-failure`-alone, so `parseRetryOn` SPLITS the 42.1-fused `connect-failure`/`reset` into `retryConnectFail` + a new `retryReset` (the 5th `retryOnBits` value; `matches` localOrigin arm `(retryConnectFail|retryReset)` preserves 42.1 dial-refusal-under-both). **The discriminator is RACE-ROBUST** (an IMPL-discovered refinement over the SPEC's simpler `errors.Is(attemptCtx.Err(), DeadlineExceeded)`, which was reproducibly racy under `-race`: the H1 driver propagates the child deadline onto the upstream socket, so the socket-I/O-timeout-broken read can return BEFORE context's timer stamps `Err()`): `timedOut := perTryTimeout>0 && ctx.Err()==nil && <failure-guard> && (errors.Is(attemptCtx.Err(), DeadlineExceeded) || !time.Now().Before(attemptDeadline))` where `<failure-guard>` is `resp.localOrigin` (H1) / `resp.localOrigin || resp.Status==0` (H2 — the timeout manifests as the `Status:0` sentinel, localOrigin=false) so a clean upstream reply is NEVER misclassified, the wall-clock fallback is monotonic-clock-immune to the timer lag, and it is read BEFORE the explicit `cancel()` (NOT defer — no timer-leak); the PARENT-alive guard preserves the 42.1 H2 `Status:0` client-cancel-not-retried invariant. The `per_try_timeout` is parsed in `parseRetryPolicy` (`hcm/config.go`, guarded `.AsDuration()`) onto a WIDENED `NewRetryPolicy` (6th param); `≤0`/unset ⇒ no bound (byte-stable), a NEGATIVE value ⇒ a route-scoped reject (`route: %q: retry_policy: per_try_timeout must not be negative`, the shared `ErrMsgPerTryTimeoutNegative` const). The **`upstream_rq_per_try_timeout` counter** is the 6th `retryStats` member via `EnsureRetryStats` (`cluster.go`) — registered on EVERY retry cluster (emit-0 where no `per_try_timeout`), ++ per timed-out attempt. The driver code is UNCHANGED (the H1 driver already `SetDeadline`s the socket `router.go:659-661`; the H2 driver already returns `Status:0` on a ctx deadline `router_h2.go:111`); NO `circuitbreaker.go` change. **Byte-identical when `per_try_timeout ≤ 0`** (the 77→78 differential is the regression anchor). DEPARTURE (D-S422A-8): the H1-records-a-local-origin-502-on-timeout vs H2-records-none asymmetry (the `0076` asserts neither `upstream_rq_502`/`upstream_rq_5xx`). Differential **`0076-per-try-timeout`** (cross-side: a SINGLE held `BlockingHoldResponder` host past a small `per_try_timeout` ⇒ deterministic exhaustion to a final 504; `upstream_rq_per_try_timeout==N+1` + `upstream_rq_retry==N` + `_retry_limit_exceeded==1` + `upstream_rq_total==N+1`, cross-side-EXACT [single host, offset-irrelevant]; `/__release` drains the parked attempts; NO new BackendKind); 2 `-count=1` deliberate breaks (A: per_try_timeout not threaded ⇒ the held read hangs to a transport timeout; B: `IncUpstreamRqPerTryTimeout` no-op ⇒ the cross-side delta assert fails) + 20/20 flake-free. ADR-0250 §Decision/§Consequences landed IN-PLACE per ADR-0044 (refined to reflect the as-built race-robust discriminator). As-built counts: stat surface **1173 → 1181** (+8 = +2 on `0075`'s two retry clusters + +6 for `0076`'s new `c_ptt` cluster) / fixtures **77 → 78** (`0076`) / fuzzers **42** (UNCHANGED) / BackendKind tail **36** (UNCHANGED — REUSE `BlockingHoldResponder`) / DECISIONS tail **ADR-0249 → ADR-0250** (next-free **ADR-0251**). ZERO new packages + ZERO new go.mod modules. **Row 42 STAYS `in-progress`** (the three-leg row flips `done` only when ALL THREE legs 42.1+42.2a+42.2b land — ADR-0106 + `reference_roadmap_split_phase_row_done`); the 42.2a IMPL records the leg LANDED, 42.2b hedging is the remaining leg. The Upstream-robustness family STAYS OPEN. **Next → the 42.2b (hedging) BRAINSTORM** (`superpowers:brainstorming` — the concurrent `hedgeExecutor` + `hedge_on_per_try_timeout` + the `initial_requests`/`additional_request_chance` fan-out over the 42.2a per-attempt deadline; ADR-0251). - **prior active-phase:** `phase 42.1 (retries-and-hedging) IMPL done` (2026-06-20) — the 13-task TDD PLAN executed end-to-end subagent-driven (fresh subagent per task + two-stage review), the six-gate GREEN, the full **77-dir differential GREEN** (lifecycle-state 3 → 4). As built: **ADR-0249** — HTTP retries (`RouteAction.retry_policy` / `VirtualHost` field-16 fallback) as a re-attempt loop WRAPPING the existing single-attempt driver: the project's FIRST request-replay control plane + its FIRST feature that RECOVERS a single request. A `retryExecutor` (`internal/filter/http/router/retry.go` — the `retry_on` tokenizer + `retryOnBits` bitset + the `matches(status, localOrigin)` classifier [`localOrigin` distinguishes a synthesized connect/reset from a 5xx RESPONSE] + the `NewRetryPolicy` ctor + the exponential full-jitter backoff [base 25ms / max 10×base; `max_interval < base_interval` reject]) wrapping `doH1ClusterAction` (`router.go`) / `doH2ClusterAction` (`router_h2.go`) + the weighted path (`router_weighted.go`): the `retry_policy` parsed in `buildRouterAction` (`hcm/config.go`) with the `VirtualHost` fallback threaded `buildRouteTable`→`buildAction`→`buildRouterAction`, carried on `clusterRouteAction` (`hcm/actions.go`), the `rp *RetryPolicy` threaded through the `H{1,2}ClusterAction` signatures; on a retriable outcome with `num_retries` (default 1) + `retry_budget` slots remaining it sleeps the backoff, replays the already-buffered request body (the ADR-0076 1 MiB buffer; the HCM 413s an over-cap body BEFORE the router, so the replay source is always ≤1MiB — NO over-cap guard needed), and re-invokes the driver (re-running CB admission + `RecordUpstreamResult` per attempt). The **+5 `upstream_rq_retry*` counters** (`upstream_rq_retry`/`_retry_success`/`_retry_limit_exceeded`/`_retry_backoff_exponential`/`_retry_backoff_ratelimited`) register SCOPED to retry-policy-bearing clusters via the lazy idempotent `Cluster.EnsureRetryStats()` (a departure — keeps non-retry clusters byte-stable; +5 per retry cluster ⇒ +10 for `0075`'s two). The **`retry_budget` activation** on the phase-41 `circuitBreaker`: a cluster-level `activeRetries atomic.Int64` + `tryAcquireRetry`/`releaseRetry` (`activeRetries < max(min_retry_concurrency, ⌈budget_percent% × activeRequests⌉)`; defaults 20%/3) flips the 2 phase-41 emit-0 stats LIVE (`circuit_breakers.<priority>.rq_retry_open` gauge + `upstream_rq_retry_overflow` counter), distinct from the static-cap `upstream_rq_retry_limit_exceeded`. **Byte-identical when no `retry_policy`** (a nil-guard — the 76-subtest differential is the regression anchor). The **AMEND-RT amendments** held as-built: (RT1) the enforced `retry_on` subset {5xx, gateway-error, connect-failure, reset, retriable-status-codes(+list)} distinguishes TRANSPORT failures from 5xx RESPONSES; parse-accept-defer {retriable-headers, envoy-ratelimited, grpc-*}; unknown tokens IGNORED (no PGV). (RT2) the +5 counters SCOPED + the 2 phase-41 stats flip LIVE. (RT4) the reference RANDOMIZES the RR initial offset ⇒ the EXHAUSTION arm is cross-side-EXACT (single 503 host: `upstream_rq_retry==N` + `limit_exceeded==1` + `total==N+1` + final 503), the RECOVER arm asserts the offset-invariant `downstream_rq_2xx==K`. (CB2) a `max_requests` overflow increments **`upstream_rq_pending_overflow`** — the SAME counter as `max_pending_requests`; there is NO `upstream_rq_overflow` (the two request budgets differ only by gauge `rq_open` vs `rq_pending_open`). (CB3) stat surface **+14 → 1163** — the full parity block: 10 per-priority `*_open` gauges (`cx_open`/`cx_pool_open`/`rq_open`/`rq_pending_open`/`rq_retry_open` × {default, high}) + 4 cluster overflow counters (`upstream_cx_overflow`/`upstream_cx_pool_overflow`/`upstream_rq_pending_overflow`/`upstream_rq_retry_overflow`), scoped to `circuit_breakers` clusters (existing fixtures unaffected; a recorded departure — the reference emits the overflow counters on ALL clusters); only `default.rq_open` + `upstream_rq_pending_overflow` go LIVE, the other 12 emit 0 (the AMEND-OD3-4 emit-0-for-parity precedent); `remaining_*` gauges DEFERRED (`track_remaining`-gated). (CB4) overflow ⇒ `503` + `UO` flag + `upstream_reset_before_response_started{overflow}`; envoy-go emits the 503 via the existing local-reply path + sets `UO` where the `%RESPONSE_FLAGS%` accesslog surface allows (a PLAN/IMPL pin); the differential asserts the robust 503-status + stats pair, NOT the access-log line. (CB5) effective defaults 1024/1024/1024/3/∞ (via the `remaining_*` gauges) — `max_requests` defaults 1024 ⇒ the breaker is OFF unless explicitly small. (CB6) the PGV reject surface is THIN — only `Thresholds.priority` enum-range + `RetryBudget.budget_percent ∈ [0,100]` bite (budget `UInt32Value`s unbounded; duplicate-priority + `per_host_thresholds` accepted); envoy-go mirrors the 2 PGV rejects + adds a strict duplicate-priority reject + silent-ignores `per_host_thresholds`; NO new fuzzer (config-parse, not wire-decode). (CB7) +1 BackendKind `BlockingHoldResponder` (35 → 36 — holds a request until the driver releases) for the deterministic budget-fill. **Architecture (ADR-0248):** a per-priority `circuitBreaker` counter struct on `Cluster` (`cluster.go:85`); a synchronous `tryAcquireRequest(DEFAULT)` at router admission (`do{H1,H2}ClusterAction` — `router.go:588`/`router_h2.go:57`, before the upstream call) + a `defer releaseRequest` on completion (endpoint-free, NOT piggybacked on `RecordUpstreamResult`); fail-fast 503-UO + `upstream_rq_pending_overflow`++ + `rq_open`=1 on exhaustion; NO background goroutine; byte-identical when no `circuit_breakers`. The 40.1-reserved `UpstreamResult.LocalOriginErr` field made LIVE — populated at the five router connection-failure sites (H1 acquire-503/write-502/read-502, H2 dial-502/roundtrip-502; live drivers only) with the picked endpoint surfaced from the shared `Cluster.Dial`/`AcquireH1`/`DialH2` connect-failure paths (`AcquireH1` widened to `(*PooledH1Conn, Endpoint, error)`) + the `(Endpoint).IsZero()` no-host-no-attribution guard (`Endpoint` carries a `Metadata` map → NOT comparable). +4 stat registrations (1137 → **1141**): `ejections_{detected,enforced}_consecutive_gateway_failure` + `ejections_{detected,enforced}_consecutive_local_origin_failure` (the 40.1 double-count EXTENDED — a gateway/local-origin eject bumps `ejections_enforced_total` too; emitted unconditionally on any outlier cluster). Two new parse-reject arms (`enforcing_consecutive_{gateway,local_origin}_failure > 100`). The `0069` `StatsAsserter` WIDENED to cross-assert `ejections_detected_consecutive_gateway_failure` (the 40.1 AMEND-OD4 gateway-detected exclusion LIFTED — `0069` routing/ejection UNCHANGED). Differentials `0070-outlier-detection-consecutive-gateway-failure` (a 503 host ejected via the gateway detector; cross-side gateway-stat parity + `detected_consecutive_5xx == 0`; cluster `c_gw`, `PerHostBackendKind` + `HTTP503Responder` 35) + `0071-outlier-detection-local-origin` (a dead/connect-refused host ejected via the local-origin detector under split=true; cross-side local-origin-stat parity + `detected_consecutive_5xx == 0`; cluster `c_lo`, the `allocDeadPort` dead-host mechanism, `BackendCount()==2`); both poll-`ejections_active`-to-converge + warmup + delta-counter; 2 `-count=1` deliberate breaks each; `0070` 20/20 + `0071` 20/20 flake-free (the split=false local-origin-as-5xx arm is a `record`-level unit test). REUSES the ADR-0242 `clusterHealth`/`hostHealth` registry + the ADR-0243 build-time-injected health-aware LB pick; ADDS one genuinely-new seam — a per-request upstream-outcome callback `cluster.RecordUpstreamResult(ep, UpstreamResult{StatusCode, LocalOriginErr})` from the H1/H2 router back to the cluster (the response status is observed in the router today but never threaded back). **Q-decisions:** (Q-subject) outlier detection (chosen over circuit breakers / retries+hedging / connection-pooling + the 3 now-unblocked health-gated LB candidates — it composes most directly with the phase-39 host health-state primitive: passive vs active health); (Q-scope) **full** outlier detection (all detector variants); (Q-split) **3 legs by detector class** — **40.1** = the passive seam + the ejection lifecycle + the single `consecutive_5xx` detector + the LB-filter extension (`isHealthy → available`) + `0069` [the minimal keystone, mirroring 39.1's single-HTTP-codec keystone]; **40.2** = the other consecutive detectors (`consecutive_gateway_failure` + `consecutive_local_origin_failure` + `split_external_local_origin_errors`); **40.3** = the statistical detectors (`success_rate` + `failure_percentage`) + a NEW per-interval cross-host mean/stddev aggregation runtime. **Architecture (Approach A → ADR-0245):** ejection is a SEPARATE per-host sub-state (new fields `ejected`/`unejectAtNanos`/`ejectCount`/`consec5xx` on `hostHealth`), DISTINCT from the active-HC `healthy` bit (outlier recovery is TIME-based — `base_ejection_time × num_ejections` linear backoff — not consecutive-success); `isHealthy`/`membership_healthy` KEEP their 39.1 active-HC-only meaning; the LB pick filters on `available = isHealthy && !isEjected` (the six constructs move their predicate `isHealthy → available`; byte-identical when no `outlier_detection`). The `UpstreamResult` seam struct is FORWARD-COMPATIBLE + fires on EVERY request so 40.2/40.3 don't re-touch the router (the 39.2 re-signature-churn lesson). **1 (possibly 2) ADRs anticipated:** ADR-0245 (the passive seam + the ejection dimension + the `available` LB-pick extension + the `consecutive_5xx` detector & ejection lifecycle); §Context at the 40.1 SPEC, body at the 40.1 IMPL per ADR-0044. Differential `0069-outlier-detection-consecutive-5xx` (a cluster {2 healthy backends, 1 always-5xx backend} + `outlier_detection{consecutive_5xx, base_ejection_time, max_ejection_percent}`; TRAFFIC-driven ejection — drive 5xx requests, POLL `/stats` until `ejections_active` converges on both sides [the `0066` poll-to-converge + warmup + delta-counter pattern, `reference_health_check_propagation_warmup`], then assert routing concentrates on the healthy hosts + cross-side `ejections_*` parity; 2 deliberate breaks; a time-based un-eject/recovery arm is a candidate-defer). **ROADMAP row 40 registers `in-progress`** (a flat family row WITH a pre-authorized 40.1/40.2/40.3 by-detector-class split; it flips `done` when all three legs land — NO parent rollup per ADR-0106). - **prior active-phase:** `phase 39 (upstream-health-check) BRAINSTORM done` (2026-06-14) — the FIRST Upstream-robustness-family row and the first NEW feature family opened since §9 (Network filters) closed at phase 33 (phases 34–38 were all Load-balancing-family rows). Charters `Cluster.health_checks` (`[]core.config.core.v3.HealthCheck`, proto field 8) — **active** upstream health checking (periodic outbound probes that mark hosts healthy/unhealthy) over an HTTP + TCP + gRPC checker roster — plus the project's FIRST per-host **health-state dimension** and the health-aware LB pick that makes all six LB constructs route only to healthy hosts (with a panic-threshold fallback). The family **keystone**: the host health-state primitive (i) unblocks the open Load-balancing family's 3 health-gated candidates {locality-weighted LB, priority load balancing, panic thresholds}, and (ii) is the substrate the sibling rows {outlier detection, circuit breakers, retries + hedging, connection pooling} compose with. **Q-decisions:** (Q0) NEW family = Upstream robustness (chosen over xDS/gRPC/HTTP3 — the foundational reliability layer that unblocks the health-gated LB candidates); (Q0b) first sub-project = active health checks (the keystone — establishes the health boundary; chosen over the cleaner-to-differential retries/circuit-breakers); (Q1) checker roster = HTTP + TCP + gRPC, split by codec — **39.1** = the keystone (host health-state dimension + the cluster-level checker runtime [the project's FIRST background runtime — `Manager.StartHealthChecks(ctx)` boot start + `Manager.Drain()` cancel/wait] + the HTTP probe codec + the health-aware LB across all six constructs) + the `0066-health-check-http` differential; **39.2** = the TCP + gRPC checkers reusing the 39.1 substrate; (Q2) health-aware LB = **Approach A: pick-time filter + panic fallback** (LB built once over all hosts; `Pick` skips unhealthy + re-picks; ring_hash/maglev walk-to-next-healthy preserving key stability; `healthy_panic_threshold` default 50% fallback-to-all), with the health view **build-time-injected** (host health is CLUSTER state, NOT a per-request `Pick` widening — the deliberate CONTRAST with the ADR-0235 hash-key / ADR-0239 subset-match ctx-carry pick-inputs; the `Pick` signature + exported `Cluster` surface stay BYTE-STABLE; behavior-neutral when all healthy → every existing fixture stays byte-identical). A NEW `health_checks` parse-reject surface (envoy-go's own byte-stable rejects; D-HC2). Differential `0066-health-check-http` (a cluster with a LIVE + a DEAD unbound host; POLL `/stats` until the dead host is unhealthy on BOTH sides [no fixed sleep], THEN assert 100% of traffic on the live host + cross-side `health_check.*`/`membership_*` parity; 3 `-count=1` deliberate breaks; ≥20-run flake-free). **2 ADRs anticipated:** ADR-0242 (the host health-state dimension + the active-HC runtime) + ADR-0243 (the health-aware LB pick — Approach A); the phase-36/38 two-ADR shape; §Context at SPEC, bodies at IMPL per ADR-0044. The phase-38 SPEC (prior commit `f52a8ea`) executed the §11 D-SS1..D-SS7 empirical pins live + anchored the ADR-0239/0240 §Context drafts + CONSUMED the FINAL 38.1/38.2 split. **SPEC-firmed deltas vs the BRAINSTORM:** the parse-reject surface is **EMPTY** — the only reference reject (`lb_subset_config` + `lb_policy: CLUSTER_PROVIDED` → `cluster: LB policy CLUSTER_PROVIDED cannot be combined with lb_subset_config`) is ALREADY covered by envoy-go's pre-existing unsupported-policy gate, so ZERO new reject arm and the `Cluster.LbPolicy` supported-list + `TestManager_Error_UnsupportedLBPolicy` are UNTOUCHED (PGV imposes nothing beyond 3 enum-range checks; empty keys / empty selectors / duplicate selectors / fallback-default mismatches ALL accept); `envoy.lb` value-equality is double-valued for numbers (int 1 ≡ 1.0 — no canonicalization) with an exact key-set match; stat surface anticipated **1121 → 1125** (+4 `lb_subsets_*`: `active` gauge + `created`/`selected`/`fallback` counters — the reference's `active`/`created` carry a **33× magnitude artifact** envoy-go does NOT mirror, pinning the ×1 distinct-subset count; only `selected`/`fallback` [request-counted] cross-side-equal in `0064`); the `lb_subsets_selected`/`fallback` counters are the FIRST stats a `loadBalancer` Inc's from its own `Pick` path (injected at register; ADR-0024 noted, not amended). **ROADMAP row 38 STAYS `in-progress`** (it flips at the 38.1 IMPL six-gate — a flat family row, NO parent rollup per ADR-0106). Charters the project's **FIFTH LB construct** and the family's **FIRST wrapper**: `Cluster.lb_subset_config` (`envoy.config.cluster.v3`) — **NOT a `Cluster.LbPolicy` enum value** (unlike the five incumbents roundRobin/leastRequest/randomLB/ringHashLB/maglevLB) but a wrapper triggered by the presence of `lb_subset_config`, that selects a subset of endpoints matching the route's `metadata_match` and **delegates to the cluster's `lb_policy` as the child policy run within that subset**. Picked from the 4-candidate roster (Q0) as **the only candidate decoupled from the absent health-checking boundary** (the other three — locality-weighted / priority / panic — are health-gated and belong after the Upstream-robustness family). As chartered: a `subsetLB` wrapper (`internal/cluster/subset.go` — enumerate subsets per selector × distinct endpoint-metadata value-tuples; a child `loadBalancer` per subset built by the existing policy factory — **ALL FIVE child policies supported** [Q1=A], the hashKey passing through to a hash-policy child; resolve the route match → subset → delegate `child.Pick`; the cluster-level fallback {NO_FALLBACK/ANY_ENDPOINT/DEFAULT_SUBSET} + `default_subset`) over a NEW `Endpoint.Metadata` dimension (the `envoy.lb` namespace captured at `extractEndpoints`, `manager.go:533` — the FIRST per-endpoint dimension beyond `Host:Port`) + a SECOND LB pick-seam input (the resolved subset match widening `Pick(hashKey, hasHash)` → `Pick(hashKey, hasHash, match, hasMatch)` + `WithSubsetMatch`/`subsetMatchFrom`, the ADR-0235 hash-key STYLE TEMPLATE) + a NEW HTTP producer plane (`RouteAction.metadata_match["envoy.lb"]` attached to `clusterRouteAction` — `actions.go:201`). The manager wraps-after-switch (subset wraps the `lb_policy` child — NOT a new `case`). **NO lb_policy reject-text change** (subset is not an enum value — the phase-34/35/36/37 doubly-hit reject-text-retarget lineage BREAKS; the reject surface shifts to `lb_subset_config` validation, D-SS1). **Pre-authorized 38.1/38.2 by-plane split (Q2/Q2b — the phase-36 valve):** 38.1 = subset LB on the HTTP `RouteAction.metadata_match` plane (this leg); 38.2 = `RouteAction.weighted_clusters` routing + `WeightedCluster.ClusterWeight.metadata_match` (a NEW per-request weighted-cluster-selection primitive — the router resolves a SINGLE cluster per route today; the brainstorm surfaced this hidden prerequisite). Differential `0064-lb-subset` (HTTP routes each carrying a `metadata_match` pinned to a subset over `envoy.lb`-tagged endpoints): subset affinity (every served backend ∈ the expected subset, BOTH sides — the SET-membership property; the `metadata_match` is STATIC config → NAT-transparent → TRUE cross-side) + a fallback arm + cross-side byte-equiv + a cross-side `StatsAsserter` (the `lb_subsets_*` stats). 2 ADRs anticipated: **ADR-0239** (the subset-match seam-input extension + the `RouteAction.metadata_match` producer — the seam-BUILD half, parallel to ADR-0235) + **ADR-0240** (the subset LB policy + the `Endpoint.Metadata` dimension — the policy half, parallel to ADR-0236/0238); the phase-36 two-ADR shape (contrast phases 35/37's single-ADR reuse); §Context at SPEC, bodies at IMPL per ADR-0044. Anticipated moves at the 38.1 IMPL: stat surface **1121 → ~1125** (~+4 `lb_subsets_*` stats — D-SS4 pins), fixtures **65 → 66** (`0064`; NO boot-reject dir anticipated — the parse rejects land unit-level), fuzzers UNCHANGED **42** (no wire decode — a subset-resolution property test is unit-level), BackendKind tail UNCHANGED **33** (`0064` reuses the `0063` HTTP backends), ZERO new packages + ZERO new go.mod deps. **The Load-balancing family stays OPEN** — **3** candidates remain after 38 {locality-weighted LB, priority load balancing, panic thresholds} (all health-gated).
